HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001294/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_02dafed10f351d9bfd94","text":"3. **A gift-and-return circuit without reducing generosity to payment.** `ك ر م:B008` supplies a gift offered with return or recompense in view, while `ك ر م:B001` preserves non-reductive noble generosity. The Rabb gives reading and knowledge (96:3–5), the human imagines independence (96:6–7), and `الرجعى` returns to the Rabb (96:8). The gift branch’s role is to make reception answerable. The packet supplies giving and return; I infer that the return entails response to the gift, not that the gift was a commercial exchange. `الأكرم` can thus activate both sheer bounty and bounty that creates responsibility.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B001","root_001294/B001","root_001294/B008"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_2234bd801d9a5986fa51","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:3:2\",\"root_000532\",[\"B001\"]],[\"96:3:3\",\"root_001294\",[\"B001\",\"B008\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Cömert armağan karşılıksızlığını yitirmeden sorumluluk doğurur; Rabbine dönüş, alınan nimete verilecek cevap hâline gelir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001294/B001","root_001294/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3631f6fa2fcd235f4c39","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:3:3\",\"root_001294\",[\"B001\",\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"En cömert oluş, insanın yeterlilik iddiasını daha baştan eşitsiz bir üstünlük yarışına çevirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B002","root_001294/B001","root_001294/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_57b0c8eb21ed12aa5326","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"96:3:2\",\"root_000532\",[\"B002\"]],[\"96:3:3\",\"root_001294\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Rabbin cömertliği, öğretimi yağmur gibi gelişme sağlayan bir besine çeviren aşamalı yetiştiricilik olarak belirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_61e13885f0c9b76eda86","text":"96:17-18 made the generous Lord's command contrast with rival summonses: the human party calls its council, while the divine side calls repelling agents. The surprise is that `الأكرم` frames even the later contest as asymmetrical; the human call cannot match the earlier gift of teaching.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001294/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_642a6ea887b1b00939b4","text":"96:6–7 corrected a simple “abundant giving” reading: generosity can be received and then misrecognized as self-possession. 96:8 unexpectedly activated the recompense-bearing gift branch `ك ر م:B008` and joined it to the Rabb’s ownership, so bounty became answerable without ceasing to be `ك ر م:B001` noble generosity.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B002","root_001294/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_68987a0e1c02603462e6","text":"2. **Generosity as cultivation.** `ك ر م:B001` supplies praiseworthy generosity, while `ك ر م:B002` supplies rain-bearing bounty and fertile growth; `ر ب ب:B002` supplies stage-by-stage nurture. Creation from attachment precedes the line and teaching by pen follows it. The growth branch makes `الأكرم` the productive condition under which a dependent being develops into a knower. I infer that teaching plays the role of rain-like provision in this local mechanism. The superlative therefore evokes not only lavish giving but cultivation that makes latent capacity grow.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B001","root_001210/B001","root_001211/B001","root_001294/B001","root_001294/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6c93ad7474da1f90819a","text":"1. The repeated imperative is intensified by generosity rather than by threat. Evidence: `ق ر ء B001/B002` recitation as gathered speech; `ر ب ب B001/B002` authority and nurture; `ك ر م B001` praiseworthy nobility/generosity. Structural cue: this second `ٱقْرَأْ` is not followed by “created” but by `وَرَبُّكَ ٱلْأَكْرَمُ`. Mechanism: the second command grounds recitation in the Lord's noble giving, preparing 96:4-5 where teaching gives what the human did not know. Abductive move: the packet supplies generosity and teaching sequence; I infer that the repeated command is supported by benefaction. Reading change: repetition does not merely insist; it reveals the reading act as response to abundant giving. 2. `الأكرم` also activates rivalry and outdoing. Evidence: `ك ر م B006` rivalry in generosity/outdoing, `س م و B007` rivaling by vying upward, and later `غ ن ي B001` self-sufficiency. Structural cue: the human later “sees himself self-sufficient,” but the Lord is already marked as most noble/generous. Mechanism: apparent human sufficiency becomes a failed competition with divine generosity. Abductive move: the packet gives superlative `الأكرم` and later self-sufficiency; I infer a contest over who is source of sufficiency. Reading change: “most generous” also reads as “unmatchable,” preempting human boasting.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_90181a07a1ddddd583b9","text":"4. **The Rabb as both cultivator and terminal authority.** `ر ب ب:B002` supplies nurture and completion, while `ر ب ب:B001` supplies lordship and ownership. The same `ربك` that accompanies reading here reappears as the destination of `الرجعى` in 96:8. I infer that return completes the developmental relation begun by nurture. The line reads with a double horizon: the Rabb generously develops the reader and remains the authority to whom that developed agency returns.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001210/B001","root_001210/B002","root_001211/B001","root_001211/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d914f94ee1b35f8a6e1e","text":"1. **A second gathering after origin has been disclosed.** The collection/recitation variant of `ق ر ء:B001` is reactivated by the exact repetition of `اقرأ` from 96:1, now after 96:2 has supplied the human’s attached origin. `ق ر ء:B002` also contains an appointed cycle variant. The second imperative’s role is iterative: the material origin must now be gathered into the act of reading. I infer from repetition across intervening content that this is a renewed pass, not redundant emphasis. The command reads as rereading after a change in self-understanding.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
